English

**[SRC: VARSITY_KA] Q20.** Make sentences with the following words:
Audacious, Alert, Aroma, Assemble, Agile
Answer: Audacious - The team made an audacious plan to
win the game in the final minute.
Alert - The alert guard dog noticed the stranger
immediately.
Aroma - The warm aroma of fresh bread filled the kitchen.
Assemble - It took us two hours to assemble the new
bookshelf.
Agile - The agile monkey climbed tall tree with ease.

<!-- Page 27 -->
**[SRC: VARSITY_KA] Q21.** Fill in the blanks with suitable determiners:
(a) I bought –––– umbrella because it was raining.
(b) There are –––– apples in the basket.
(c) She has –––– friends in this city.
(d) –––– student must submit the assignment.
(e) He spent –––– money on books.

Answer: (a) an
(b) many/several/few/some
(c) few/several/many/some/a few
(d) every/each
(e) some/little/a lot of/lots of/much/a little/all the

**[SRC: VARSITY_KA] Q22.** Write a paragraph on ‘Effects of Plastic Pollution on the
Environment’ within 100 words.
Answer: Effects of Plastic Pollution on the Environment
Plastic pollution has become one of the most serious
environmental problems in the modern world. Every year,
millions of tons of plastic waste enter rivers, seas, and land,
harming wildlife and ecosystems. Marine animals often
mistake plastic for food, which can cause injury or death.
Plastic also takes hundreds of years to decompose, making
it a long-lasting threat. As it breaks down, it turns into
microplastics that contaminate water and food chains. This
pollution also affects human health and beauty of natural
environments. To reduce this problem, we must recycle,
reduce plastic use, and promote eco-friendly alternatives in
daily life.

English

**[SRC: VARSITY_KA] Q20.** Make sentences with the following words:
Abyss, Afford, Anticipate, Abrupt, Absorb
Answer: Abyss – He felt as if he was staring into an endless
abyss of uncertainty.
Afford – He saved his allowance so he could afford the
new video game
Anticipate – We anticipate a warm and sunny day tomorrow.
Abrupt – The bus came to an abrupt stop at the red light.
Absorb – A dry sponge will absorb water very quickly.

**[SRC: VARSITY_KA] Q21.** Fill in the gaps with suitable determiners:
(a) –––– water in the bottle is cold.
(b) They visited –––– museum yesterday.
(c) I have read –––– books written by him.
(d) She did not make –––– mistake.
(e) We need –––– information before deciding.
Answer: (a) The ; (b) a/the
(c) many/several/some/few/a few/(numbers)/no
(d) any/a ; (e) more/some/enough

**[SRC: VARSITY_KA] Q22.** Write a paragraph on “Benefits of Online Learning in
Modern Education” within 100 words.
Answer: Benefits of Online Learning in Modern
Education
Online learning has become an important part of modern
education, offering many benefits to students. It allows
learners to study from anywhere, making education more
flexible and accessible. Students can learn at their own
pace, which helps them understand topics better and revise
difficult lessons easily. Online learning also provides access
to a wide range of resources, such as videos, e-books, and
interactive tools.
It is often more affordable than traditional classroom
education, saving time and transportation costs.
Additionally, it helps students develop digital skills that are
essential in today’s world. Overall, online learning makes
education more convenient, inclusive, and effective.

English

**[SRC: VARSITY_KA] Q20.** Make sentences with the following words:

Ambiguous, Amuse, Ascend, Anchor, Altitude

Answer: Ambiguous – His answer was so ambiguous that
no one understood what he meant.

Amuse – The funny clown amused all the kids at the party.

Ascend – The likers began to ascend the steep mountain path.

Anchor – The sailors lowered the anchor to hold the ship
steady.

Altitude – The airplane reached a high altitude above the
clouds.

**[SRC: VARSITY_KA] Q21.** Fill in the blanks with suitable determiners:

(a) There were ––––– people at the meeting

(b) He gave me –––– useful advice.

(c) ––––– children like cartoons.

(d) She has ––––– idea about the plan.

(e) We waited for ––––– hour.

Answer: (a) many/several/few/some/a few

(b) some/much ; (c) Most/Many/All

(d) no/some/an ; (e) an/one

**[SRC: VARSITY_KA] Q22.** Write a paragraph on “Role of Education in Reducing
Poverty” with 100 words.

Answer: Role of Education in Reducing Poverty

The role of education in reducing poverty is extremely
important in building a better society. Education provides
people with knowledge, skills, and opportunities to improve
their lives. When individuals are educated, they are more
likely to get better jobs and earn a stable income, which
helps lift families out of poverty. It also promotes
awareness about health, rights, and financial management.
Educated people can make better decisions and contribute
to economic growth. Moreover, education helps reduce
inequality by giving everyone a fair chance to succeed.
Therefore, investing in education is one of the most
effective ways to break the cycle of poverty.

English

**[SRC: VARSITY_KA] Q20.** Make meaningful sentences with the following words:
Allocate, Apex, Astonish, Adorn, Avert
Answer: Allocate – The manager decided to allocate a
larger budget to marketing this year.
Apex – The climber finally reached the apex of the
mountain.
Astonish – His incredible magic trick will astonish
everyone in the crowd.
Adorn – They used colorful flowers to adorn the dining
room table.
Avert – She had to avert her eyes from the bright sunlight.

**[SRC: VARSITY_KA] Q21.** Fill in the blanks with suitable determiners:
(a) ––––– boy in the class received a prize.
(b) I have ––––– patience left.
(c) There isn’t –––– sugar in the jar
(d) We spent –––– day at the beach.
(e) She invited ––––– of her classmates.

Answer: (a) Each/Every
(b) little/no/a little
(c) much/any
(d) the/a/one
(e) all/most/many

**[SRC: VARSITY_KA] Q22.** Write a paragraph on “Preservation of Traditional
Culture in a Globalized World” within 100 words.

Answer: Preservation of Traditional Culture in a
Globalized World
The preservation of traditional culture in a globalized world
is very important for maintaining a society’s identity and
heritage. Globalization has made the world more connected,
allowing people to share ideas, languages, and lifestyles.
However, it has also led to the loss of many traditional
customs, values, and languages. Younger generations are
often more influenced by foreign cultures through media
and technology. To preserve traditional culture, people
should actively practice their customs, celebrate festivals,
and learn their native languages. Schools and families also
play a key role in teaching cultural values. By balancing
modernization with tradition, societies can remain
culturally rich and diverse.

### Cross-Referenced Chapter Mapping for Week_01 Syllabus:
The questions in the DU 'Ka' Question Bank corresponding to the topics covered in Week_01 are found in every annual paper under the following subjects:
- **[SRC: DU_QB] Physics**:
  - Chapter 02 (Vector / ভেক্টর): Dot & cross product, resultant, projection, unit vectors, work done by force vector.
  - Chapter 03 (Dynamics / গতিবিদ্যা): Equations of motion, projectile motion, circular motion velocity & acceleration, v-t and s-t graph interpretations.
- **[SRC: DU_QB] Chemistry**:
  - 1st Paper Chapter 01 (Safe Lab Practices / ল্যাবরেটরির নিরাপদ ব্যবহার): Cleaning mixtures, hazard pictograms, measuring apparatus precision.
  - 2nd Paper Chapter 03 (Quantitative Chemistry / পরিমাণগত রসায়ন): Molar volume at STP, stoichiometry, acid-base neutralization titrations, redox balancing, ppm concentration.
- **[SRC: DU_QB] Higher Mathematics**:
  - 1st Paper Chapter 03 (Straight Lines / সরলরেখা): Coordinates, distance, section formulas, slope, line equations, perpendicular distance, concurrency.
  - 2nd Paper Chapter 02 (Linear Programming / যোগাশ্রয়ী প্রোগ্রাম): Feasible region, objective function maximization/minimization.
- **[SRC: DU_QB] Biology**:
  - 2nd Paper Chapter 04 (Circulation & Blood / রক্ত ও সংবহন): Blood corpuscles, heart anatomy, cardiac cycle, pacemaker, arterial system.
  - 2nd Paper Chapter 05 (Respiration / শ্বসন ও শ্বাসক্রিয়া): Respiratory system anatomy, oxygen and carbon dioxide transport.
